# STALL_LOG.md — video-budget stall audit trail

Budget refusals (`video_daily_budget_exhausted`) are logged here, NOT in LEARNING_LOG.md
(a refusal is not a lesson). One short line per stalled run: date/time, factory,
cumulative refusal count for the outage, queued numbers + durable prompt paths
(nothing consumed, nothing retired). When the budget resets, the resuming factory
appends ONE line to LEARNING_LOG.md noting the outage window and the resume.

## Outage 2026-10-15 ~01:45 EDT → (open as of 12:45 EDT)

- Rolling 24h video quota exhausted after the 01:45 EDT ships (B-962..B-964); longest outage of the night.
- Refusals 1–63 logged in LEARNING_LOG.md lines ~1904–1989 (historical; per-run bookkeeping moved here from run 64 on).
- Queued, durable, fire verbatim sequentially on reset:
  - Bumpers: B-965 "The Twisted Rope" + B-966 "The Signaled Arm" + B-967 "The Raked Salt" — slate/fire_b965_20261015_0200.txt, fire_b966_20261015_0200.txt, fire_b967_20261015_0200.txt
  - Commercials: AD-178 "Baled Tight" + AD-183 "Folded in Thirds" — slate/fire_ad178_20261015_0149.txt, slate/fire_ad183_20261015_0149.txt

- Nothing consumed, nothing retired (budget events never consume claims or retire shapes).
